# model-registry-operator: Agent Context

Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- model-registry-operator (also known as\: Model Registry operator) is a Kubernetes component.
- managing 1 CRD(s)\: ModelRegistry.
- deploying 4 workload(s) (model-registry-operator-controller-manager, catalog-controller-manager, aihub-controller-manager, template-value) with 5 container(s).
- watching 20 resource type(s).
- exposing 5 service(s) on ports\: 443, 5432, 8443.
- with 4 webhook(s) (2 validating, 1 mutating).
- protected by 10 network policy/policies.
- runtime dependencies\: PostgreSQL.
- defining 9 API spec type(s) across 43 struct(s) with 208 total fields.
- with 131 operator config constants (28 image references).
- following 6 reconciliation steps across 3 controller(s).
- reporting 16 status condition types.
- with 5 platform-conditional resource paths.
- with 5 ingress route(s).
- integrates with management relationship via labels/annotations.
- deploys OAuth proxy sidecar for authentication.
- deploys kube-rbac-proxy sidecar for RBAC-based authorization.
- kustomize overlays\: config/certmanager, config/crd, config/default, config/jobs-async-upload, config/manager, config/overlays/aihub, config/overlays/aihub/rbac, config/overlays/catalog, config/overlays/catalog/rbac, config/overlays/odh/core, config/overlays/odh/extras, config/overlays/odh, config/overlays/odh/network-policies, config/overlays/openshift, config/prometheus, config/rbac, config/webhook.
- mounts 2 ConfigMap(s) as volumes\: odh-trusted-ca-bundle, template-value-kube-rbac-proxy-config.
